First, let ⟦수식⟧̅ be the modified entropy and use Lagrangian multipliers to define this modified entropy as ⟦수식⟧̅ = ⟦수식⟧+ (⟦수식⟧+ 1) ቈ⟦수식⟧(⟦수식⟧)⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ −1቉+ ⟦수식⟧ቈ⟦수식⟧(⟦수식⟧)⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ −⟦수식⟧቉ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ Equation 5 If we differentiate Equation 5 with respect to f(x) and combine terms we get ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧−lnሾ⟦수식⟧(⟦수식⟧)] + ⟦수식⟧+ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ = 0 Equation 6 Since the only way for the integral in Equation 6 to be zero is for the integrand to be zero then ⟦수식⟧(⟦수식⟧) = ⟦수식⟧∑ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ Equation 7 Equation 7 is the analytic form for the maximum entropy density function. So we now use the constraints on the problem to determine the values of ⟦수식⟧. 식 C-6~C-7 · 최대 엔트로피 밀도함수의 해석 형태

기호와 하첨자

기호의미하첨자 읽는 법
f(x)최대 엔트로피 분포(MED)의 밀도함수-
λk모멘트 제약에서 정해지는 계수k = 1…N
왜 이 식을 쓰나

식 5를 f(x)로 미분해 극값 조건을 얻고, 이를 만족하는 밀도함수의 닫힌 형태를 얻기 위해서입니다. 원문은 식 7을 "analytic form for the maximum entropy density function"이라고 부릅니다.

어떤 조건에 쓰나

식 2–4 제약 아래의 최대 엔트로피 문제.

적용 방법

피적분함수가 0이라는 조건에서 식 7을 얻습니다. 이후 제약으로 λk를 정합니다(식 8–10).

주의

"적분이 0이려면 피적분함수가 0이어야 한다"는 설명은 일반적으로는 성립하지 않고 변분의 임의성 조건을 생략한 것입니다(오류 대장 #41, Codex 지적). 식 7 자체와 설명의 결함을 구분하세요.

식 C-8~C-10 · 승수 λ를 정하는 연립 방정식

기호와 하첨자

기호의미하첨자 읽는 법
λ0정규화 상수(식 3으로 정해짐)0 = 정규화 항
λ1..m모멘트 제약 승수m = 모멘트 수
mii차 모멘트-
왜 이 식을 쓰나

식 7의 계수 λk를 구속조건에서 정하기 위해서입니다. 식 7을 식 3에 대입하고 e−λ0를 곱해 로그를 취하면 식 8이 나옵니다.

어떤 조건에 쓰나

모멘트 m1..m이 주어졌을 때.

적용 방법

식 8을 λi로 미분해 식 9, 식 4와 식 7으로 식 10을 얻으면 m개 연립방정식으로 λ1…λm을 구하고, 이를 식 8에 넣어 λ0를 구합니다.

주의

C.8에서는 지수항 문제 때문에 x 범위를 [0,1]로 재정규화하며 CDF가 하한·상한에 따라 달라집니다(예: 항복 150±15 MPa, ±8σ에서는 정규와 동일, ±2σ에서는 달라짐, Figure 122–124). 변환 설명(Dirac 함수 필요)은 적용 조건이 부족합니다(#42).

Adams, et al, Dakota, A Multilevel Parallel Object-Oriented Framework for Design Optimization, Parameter Estimation, Uncertainty Quantification, and Sensitivity Analysis, Version 5.3.1+ Theory Manual, SAND2011-9106, May 2013.[PDF 183 / 부록 쪽]C-16 these values are known then Equation 8 can be used to solve for ⟦수식⟧ which gives us all of the parameters needed for the maximum entropy distribution. An example of using the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED) for a data set for the yield strength that is characterized by only two moments is provided in the following section. C.8 MAXIMUM ENTROPY DISTRIBUTION EXAMPLE FOR YIELD STRENGTH The fitting of the maximum entropy distribution is problematic from the standpoint of the exponential term in Equation 7. To deal with this we renormalize the range of x to [0, 1]. The reason that it is problematic is that trying to transform the interval from [−∞, ∞] to [0, 1] is not possible unless one utilizes the Dirac function which provides no probabilistic information. But this renormalization process means that the cumulative distribution function (CDF) changes depending on the lower and upper bounds. To illustrate this we examine Figure 122. In this case the CDF is between the normal distribution theory and the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED). We examine the case of the yield strength that is defined by a normal distribution. In this case we assume that sufficient data has been collected on the yield strength of the material that we are confident that a normal distribution adequately fits the data. In this case the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED) and the normal distribution give identical results as shown in Figure 122. Figure 122 Comparison of the normal distribution and the maximum entropy distribution for the yield strength when the lower and upper bounds are eight standard deviations from the mean Of course for the MED distribution we must supply upper and lower bounds in order to obtain the Lagrangian coefficients. In the fitting of the coefficients we assumed that the lower bound was 8

항복 강도 예: 정규 vs MED vs 절단 정규

  • 지수항 문제 때문에 x 범위를 [0, 1]로 재정규화. 구간 [−∞, ∞]를 [0, 1]로 바꾸는 것은 Dirac 함수 없이는 불가능하며 확률 정보를 주지 못함 [주의: 원문의 주장입니다. 일반 수학 명제로는 성립하지 않습니다 — 예: y=1/2+arctan(x)/π는 실수축을 (0,1)로 일대일 대응시킵니다. 실제 쟁점은 변환 자체가 아니라 유한 경계를 정해 MED를 맞출 때 CDF가 경계에 따라 달라진다는 점입니다]. 재정규화 때문에 CDF가 하한·상한에 따라 달라짐
  • Figure 122: 항복 강도 정규분포(평균 150 MPa, 표준편차 15 MPa)에서 하한·상한을 8σ(범위 [30, 270])로 둘 때 정규와 MED가 동일
  • Figure 123: 범위를 [120, 180](±2σ)로 두면 MED가 달라짐
  • MED가 단순 절단 정규일 것으로 생각할 수 있으나 Figure 124에서 보듯 샘플링 방식이 전혀 같지 않음. 변수 한계가 평균에서 4σ 이내일 때 정규분포를 가정하면 이 가정에 의해 편향된 샘플링 절차가 도입됨
원문 영어 (PDF 183–185, 그대로) — 위 한국어 문장이 압축·의역일 수 있으니 의심되면 여기서 확인
[PDF 183 / 부록 쪽]C-16 these values are known then Equation 8 can be used to solve for ⟦수식⟧ which gives us all of the parameters needed for the maximum entropy distribution. An example of using the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED) for a data set for the yield strength that is characterized by only two moments is provided in the following section. C.8 MAXIMUM ENTROPY DISTRIBUTION EXAMPLE FOR YIELD STRENGTH The fitting of the maximum entropy distribution is problematic from the standpoint of the exponential term in Equation 7. To deal with this we renormalize the range of x to [0, 1]. The reason that it is problematic is that trying to transform the interval from [−∞, ∞] to [0, 1] is not possible unless one utilizes the Dirac function which provides no probabilistic information. But this renormalization process means that the cumulative distribution function (CDF) changes depending on the lower and upper bounds. To illustrate this we examine Figure 122. In this case the CDF is between the normal distribution theory and the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED). We examine the case of the yield strength that is defined by a normal distribution. In this case we assume that sufficient data has been collected on the yield strength of the material that we are confident that a normal distribution adequately fits the data. In this case the Maximum Entropy Distribution (MED) and the normal distribution give identical results as shown in Figure 122. Figure 122 Comparison of the normal distribution and the maximum entropy distribution for the yield strength when the lower and upper bounds are eight standard deviations from the mean Of course for the MED distribution we must supply upper and lower bounds in order to obtain the Lagrangian coefficients. In the fitting of the coefficients we assumed that the lower bound was 8[PDF 184 / 부록 쪽]C-17 standard deviations below the mean and the upper bound was 8 standard deviations above the mean, i.e., the range of yield strengths was assumed to be [30, 270] based on a mean of 150 MPa and a standard deviation of 15 MPa. If we had assumed a range of [120, 180], i.e. a +/-2σ range then the MED distribution would be as in Figure 123. Obviously this impacts the distribution but it is necessary in order to impose lower and upper bounds. Figure 123 Comparison of the normal distribution and the maximum entropy distribution for the yield strength when the lower and upper bounds are two standard deviations from the mean Now one may think that the MED solution is just representing the truncated normal, however, this is not the case. When the standard normal, truncated normal, and MED are plotted together, as they are in Figure 124, we see that the sampling scheme is not at all the same. The assumption of the distribution of the random variable being normally distributed when the variable limits are within 4 standard deviations of the mean introduces a sampling procedure that is biased by this assumption.[PDF 185 / 부록 쪽]C-18 Figure 124 Comparison of the normal distribution, the maximum entropy distribution, and the truncated normal distribution for the yield strength when the lower and upper bounds are two standard deviations from the mean C.9 WRS ALL POINT CORRELATION The point-to-point correlation was chosen for computational efficiency in addition to maintaining a profile shape similar to the results of finite element analyses. We can examine an alternative approach in which we use the same philosophy but require all 26 points to be correlated. The restriction we implement is that all points are correlated with the same correlation coefficient. In this case we can calculate the Cholesky decomposition once for the input value of the correlation coefficient. We show the lower triangular decomposition in Applied to the stress values at the first two locations (at the ID and 4% through the thickness) this gives the sampled values of stresses with correlation. For a ⟦수식⟧ value equal to 1, ⟦수식⟧ and ⟦수식⟧ will be similar, meaning that each WRS profile will be defined by the sampling on the ID value (all the profiles will be parallels). This method is called the Cholesky decomposition and, while presented only for two variables, it can be extended to any number of variables. It is usually presented as the LU decomposition of a symmetric positive definite matrix, where L represent a Lower Triangular matrix and U its upper triangular transpose. This transformation is applied to the correlation matrix and gives in the case of a 2 x 2 correlation matrix: 1⟦수식⟧1=10⟦수식⟧1−⟦수식⟧21⟦수식⟧01−⟦수식⟧2=⟦수식⟧ An uncorrelated vector ⟦수식⟧= ቀ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ቁ can then be used to generate correlated data ⟦수식⟧∗= ⟦수식⟧ ∗ ⟦수식⟧ ∗⟦수식⟧ via a simple matrix operation: ⟦수식⟧∗= ⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧= ⟦수식⟧1 0 ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧1 −⟦수식⟧. ቀ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ቁ= ⟦수식⟧﷩⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧+ ⟦수식⟧1 −⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧﷩﷩

모든 26점에 같은 상관계수를 쓰는 대안

  • 점 간 상관은 계산 효율과 FE 해석 형상 유지를 위해 선택. 대안: 같은 철학으로 26점 모두가 같은 상관계수로 상관되도록 요구. 이 경우 입력 상관계수에 대해 Cholesky 분해를 한 번만 계산하면 됨
  • 본문 §6.1의 2×2 Cholesky 설명과 같은 식이 반복(z₃=ρz₁+√(1−ρ²)z₂, Z*=LZ). Table 5는 ρ=0.8, Table 36은 ρ=0.95의 26×26 하삼각 행렬(0.95는 반올림으로 첫 열에서 1로 표시)
  • N개 상관 변수 일반식: 상관행렬 Γ는 I≠J이면 ρ, I=J이면 1. C CT=Γ가 되도록 C를 찾고 상관 표본 Y = μ + C Z
원문 PDF 텍스트에서 이 쪽의 일부 수식·문장이 겹쳐 인쇄된 부분(Table 5 참조 문장 등)이 있어 PDF 지면으로 확인해야 합니다. 원문 C-19 문장에는 "Table 5는 상관계수 0.80, 0.95는 Table 36"이라고 적혀 있습니다.

[PDF 185 / 부록 쪽]C-18 Figure 124 Comparison of the normal distribution, the maximum entropy distribution, and the truncated normal distribution for the yield strength when the lower and upper bounds are two standard deviations from the mean C.9 WRS ALL POINT CORRELATION The point-to-point correlation was chosen for computational efficiency in addition to maintaining a profile shape similar to the results of finite element analyses. We can examine an alternative approach in which we use the same philosophy but require all 26 points to be correlated. The restriction we implement is that all points are correlated with the same correlation coefficient. In this case we can calculate the Cholesky decomposition once for the input value of the correlation coefficient. We show the lower triangular decomposition in Applied to the stress values at the first two locations (at the ID and 4% through the thickness) this gives the sampled values of stresses with correlation. For a ⟦수식⟧ value equal to 1, ⟦수식⟧ and ⟦수식⟧ will be similar, meaning that each WRS profile will be defined by the sampling on the ID value (all the profiles will be parallels). This method is called the Cholesky decomposition and, while presented only for two variables, it can be extended to any number of variables. It is usually presented as the LU decomposition of a symmetric positive definite matrix, where L represent a Lower Triangular matrix and U its upper triangular transpose. This transformation is applied to the correlation matrix and gives in the case of a 2 x 2 correlation matrix: 1⟦수식⟧1=10⟦수식⟧1−⟦수식⟧21⟦수식⟧01−⟦수식⟧2=⟦수식⟧ An uncorrelated vector ⟦수식⟧= ቀ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ቁ can then be used to generate correlated data ⟦수식⟧∗= ⟦수식⟧ ∗ ⟦수식⟧ ∗⟦수식⟧ via a simple matrix operation: ⟦수식⟧∗= ⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧= ⟦수식⟧1 0 ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧1 −⟦수식⟧. ቀ⟦수식⟧ ⟦수식⟧ቁ= ⟦수식⟧﷩⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧+ ⟦수식⟧1 −⟦수식⟧. ⟦수식⟧﷩﷩[PDF 186 / 부록 쪽]C-19 As can be seen in the above equation, the first element of the vector remains unchanged while the second (or any subsequent vectors considering that the method can be extended to larger vectors) will be affected by the correlation coefficient ⟦수식⟧ and the first element value ⟦수식⟧. Table 5 shows the resulting lower triangular distribution for a 26×26 correlation matrix when the coefficient of correlation is set to 0.8. The first column has been highlighted in red, while the diagonal was highlighted in green. They show that the sampled value at the ID contributes the most to any correlated stress (the red highlighted box represents the weight applied) and therefore strongly influences the profile generated. The next biggest contributor is, as expected, the sampled value at the location itself (green highlighted box). This feature is strongly desired for this WRS representation, as it means that the profile will be strongly affected by the sampling of the first value (WRS at the ID). It means that an efficient importance sampling can be implemented by focusing only on the sampled value at the ID (one scalar value) and not having to use importance sampling on the other 25 locations. Table 5 for a correlation coefficient of 0.80 and for a coefficient of 0.95 the matrix is shown in Table 36. 교차검증(Claude·Codex)Claude: 원문 텍스트·PDF 지면 이미지로 작성. Codex(round45, PDF 187쪽 전수 조사)와 쪽별 대조. 둘 다 일치. Table 36 반올림(#44), 배율 누락(#45), ρ<0(#46), 렌더 손상(#43) ※ Codex가 이 HTML을 역검증함(round45/codex_review.md, 표 7~34 728행 전부 일치). 이 카드에 관한 지적은 반영 완료.
C-19페이지 108 / 109원문 p.C-19 · PDF 186

식 C-11 · 상수 상관행렬의 분해와 상관 표본 Y = μ + C·Z

기호와 하첨자

기호의미하첨자 읽는 법
Γ̄상관행렬: I=J이면 1, I≠J이면 ρI, J = 점 번호
C̄C̄C̄ᵀ=Γ̄를 만족하는 하삼각 행렬(Cholesky)-
Z독립 표준정규 벡터-
μ평균 벡터-
Y상관된 표본-
왜 이 식을 쓰나

26점 모두에 같은 상관계수를 쓰면 입력 ρ에 대해 Cholesky 분해를 한 번만 계산하면 되기 때문입니다(C.9 대안).

어떤 조건에 쓰나

모든 점 쌍에 같은 ρ를 쓰는 경우. Table 5(ρ=0.80), Table 36(ρ=0.95)이 이 경우의 26×26 행렬입니다.

적용 방법

C̄를 구한 뒤 Z를 곱하고 μ를 더해 상관 표본을 만듭니다.

주의

식 Y=μ+CZ에는 표준편차 배율이 없으므로 응력 표본으로 쓰려면 본문(p.71)의 μ+φz 변환을 함께 써야 합니다(오류 대장 #45). "ρ<0인 상관행렬은 정의 불가"라는 C-20 문장은 상수 상관행렬에서 N=26이면 −1/25<ρ<1이 양의정부호이므로 과도합니다(#46, 고유값 1−ρ, 1+(N−1)ρ로 검산).

원문 영어 (PDF 186–186, 그대로) — 위 한국어 문장이 압축·의역일 수 있으니 의심되면 여기서 확인
[PDF 186 / 부록 쪽]C-19 As can be seen in the above equation, the first element of the vector remains unchanged while the second (or any subsequent vectors considering that the method can be extended to larger vectors) will be affected by the correlation coefficient ⟦수식⟧ and the first element value ⟦수식⟧. Table 5 shows the resulting lower triangular distribution for a 26×26 correlation matrix when the coefficient of correlation is set to 0.8. The first column has been highlighted in red, while the diagonal was highlighted in green. They show that the sampled value at the ID contributes the most to any correlated stress (the red highlighted box represents the weight applied) and therefore strongly influences the profile generated. The next biggest contributor is, as expected, the sampled value at the location itself (green highlighted box). This feature is strongly desired for this WRS representation, as it means that the profile will be strongly affected by the sampling of the first value (WRS at the ID). It means that an efficient importance sampling can be implemented by focusing only on the sampled value at the ID (one scalar value) and not having to use importance sampling on the other 25 locations. Table 5 for a correlation coefficient of 0.80 and for a coefficient of 0.95 the matrix is shown in Table 36. 11. 원문 내부 불일치·오류 대장

Codex가 PDF 전 쪽을 읽으며 찾은 47건 + Claude 확인 표시

오류 대장페이지 109 / 109원문 p.전체 · PDF 1–187

원문 내부의 불일치·모호·오류 47건 (쪽: PDF/인쇄)

읽는 법

원문을 고친 정답이 아니라 "원문에 이렇게 적혀 있다"는 기록입니다. 쪽은 PDF/인쇄 쪽(부록은 A-n 등)이며, 마지막 열은 Claude가 PDF 지면·텍스트·계산으로 직접 확인했는지 표시합니다.

인용한 원문 쪽인쇄 전체 (PDF 1–187)
본문 PDF 쪽 = 인쇄 쪽 + 15
교차검증(Claude·Codex)Claude: 원문 텍스트·PDF 지면 이미지로 작성. Codex(round45, PDF 187쪽 전수 조사)와 쪽별 대조. Codex 47건 중 23건은 Claude가 PDF 지면·텍스트·계산으로 직접 확인함(표 마지막 열) ※ Codex가 이 HTML을 역검증함(round45/codex_review.md, 표 7~34 728행 전부 일치). 이 카드에 관한 지적은 반영 완료.

식과 값, 설명의 근거는 위 보고서 한 편(B049)입니다. 설명 문장은 새로 썼고 원문 저작권은 EPRI에 있습니다. 원문 그림 곡선의 수치는 옮기지 않았습니다.
